Download bibliography (BibTeX / BibLaTeX): ./materials/references.bib
[EDU_BASTANI] Bastani, H., Bastani, O., Sungu, A., Ge, H., Kabakcı, Ö., & Mariman, R. (2025). Generative AI without guardrails can harm learning: Evidence from high school mathematics. Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences, 122(26), Article e2422633122. https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2422633122
https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2422633122
RCT in high-school mathematics at one Turkish school, with nearly 1,000 students. Separates assisted practice from subsequent unassisted tests; does not evaluate this workshop or these commercial tools.
[EDU_DENG] Deng, R., Jiang, M., Yu, X., Lu, Y., & Liu, S. (2025). Does ChatGPT enhance student learning? A systematic review and meta-analysis of experimental studies. Computers & Education, 227, Article 105224. https://doi.org/10.1016/j.compedu.2024.105224
https://doi.org/10.1016/j.compedu.2024.105224
Experimental and quasi-experimental studies from 2022–2024, mostly university settings. High heterogeneity (academic performance I² = 91.789%), publication bias and post-intervention assessment concerns; improved assisted output is not proof of lasting independent learning.
[EDU_KESTIN] Kestin, G., Miller, K., Klales, A., Milbourne, T., & Ponti, G. (2025). AI tutoring outperforms in-class active learning: An RCT introducing a novel research-based design in an authentic educational setting. Scientific Reports, 15(1), Article 17458. https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-97652-6
https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-97652-6
194 eligible university physics students; tutor designed for specific lessons and immediate tests. Does not establish universal or long-term chatbot effectiveness.
[EDU_HUANG] Huang, B., Xie, L., Liu, Y., Liu, X., Tang, H., Feng, X., Qiao, C., Guo, Q., Wu, C., & Bao, L. (2026). From answer machine to collaborative partner: Impact of structured generative AI training on scientific problem solving in STEM education. International Journal of STEM Education, 13(1), Article 62. https://doi.org/10.1186/s40594-026-00652-9
https://doi.org/10.1186/s40594-026-00652-9
95 students; immediate AI-assisted revision of electromagnetism questions after structured training. Does not establish durable independent learning.
[EDU_RETRIEVAL] Karpicke, J. D., & Blunt, J. R. (2011). Retrieval practice produces more learning than elaborative studying with concept mapping. Science, 331(6018), 772–775. https://doi.org/10.1126/science.1199327
https://doi.org/10.1126/science.1199327
Experiments with science texts and specific study conditions. Does not show that concept maps are useless or every generated quiz constitutes effective retrieval practice.
[EDU_SRL] Panadero, E. (2017). A review of self-regulated learning: Six models and four directions for research. Frontiers in Psychology, 8, Article 422. https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2017.00422
https://doi.org/10.3389/fpsyg.2017.00422
Theoretical review of six self-regulated learning models, not validation of this workshop; self-direction and self-regulation are related but distinct.
[TECH_EXPLAINER] Cho, A., Kim, G. C., Karpekov, A., Lee, S., Helbling, A., Hoover, B., Wang, Z. J., Kahng, M., & Chau, D. H. (2026). Transformer Explainer: Learning LLM Transformers with interactive visual explanation and experimentation. Proceedings of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems, 1–21. https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3791725
https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3791725
Complete CHI 2026 paper; arXiv v2, August 2026. Interactive resource uses GPT-2, which differs from the workshop’s Qwen model.
[TECH_TRANSFORMER] Vaswani, A., Shazeer, N., Parmar, N., Uszkoreit, J., Jones, L., Gomez, A. N., Kaiser, Ł., & Polosukhin, I. (2017). Attention is all you need. Advances in Neural Information Processing Systems, 30. https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2017/hash/3f5ee243547dee91fbd053c1c4a845aa-Abstract.html
https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2017/hash/3f5ee243547dee91fbd053c1c4a845aa-Abstract.html
Original encoder–decoder architecture; the workshop demo uses a later causal decoder.
[TECH_INSTRUCT] Ouyang, L., Wu, J., Jiang, X., Almeida, D., Wainwright, C., Mishkin, P., Zhang, C., Agarwal, S., Slama, K., Ray, A., Schulman, J., Hilton, J., Kelton, F., Miller, L., Simens, M., Askell, A., Welinder, P., Christiano, P. F., Leike, J., & Lowe, R. (2022). Training language models to follow instructions with human feedback. Advances in Neural Information Processing Systems, 35, 27730–27744. https://doi.org/10.52202/068431-2011
https://doi.org/10.52202/068431-2011
InstructGPT method, rather than a complete description of every instruction-tuned model.
[TECH_RAG] Lewis, P., Perez, E., Piktus, A., Petroni, F., Karpukhin, V., Goyal, N., Küttler, H., Lewis, M., Yih, W.-t., Rocktäschel, T., Riedel, S., & Kiela, D. (2020). Retrieval-augmented generation for knowledge-intensive NLP tasks. Advances in Neural Information Processing Systems, 33, 9459–9474. https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper/2020/hash/6b493230205f780e1bc26945df7481e5-Abstract.html
https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper/2020/hash/6b493230205f780e1bc26945df7481e5-Abstract.html
Foundational RAG research, not documentation of Notebook’s internal implementation.
[EDU_MAPS] Novak, J. D., & Cañas, A. J. (2008). The theory underlying concept maps and how to construct and use them. (Technical Report IHMC CmapTools 2006-01, Rev. 01-2008). Florida Institute for Human and Machine Cognition. https://cmap.ihmc.us/publications/researchpapers/theoryunderlyingconceptmaps.pdf
https://cmap.ihmc.us/publications/researchpapers/theoryunderlyingconceptmaps.pdf
Theoretical and practical technical report; not an efficacy trial of AI-generated diagrams.
[EDU_UNESCO] Miao, F., Shiohira, K., & Lao, N. (2024). AI competency framework for students. UNESCO. https://doi.org/10.54675/jkjb9835
https://doi.org/10.54675/jkjb9835
Competency framework, rather than experimental evidence of learning gains.
[ACC_CAST] CAST (2024). CAST Universal Design for Learning Guidelines version 3.0. https://udlguidelines.cast.org/
https://udlguidelines.cast.org/
Design guidance; not a clinical prescription or guarantee of individual benefit.
[ACC_COGA] World Wide Web Consortium (2021). Making content usable for people with cognitive and learning disabilities. (W3C Working Group Note, 29 April 2021). https://www.w3.org/TR/2021/NOTE-coga-usable-20210429/
https://www.w3.org/TR/2021/NOTE-coga-usable-20210429/
Supplemental cognitive-accessibility guidance, rather than an additional normative WCAG AA requirement.
[ACC_AID] Associazione Italiana Dislessia (n.d.). Gli strumenti compensativi [Compensatory tools]. https://www.aiditalia.org/gli-strumenti-compensativi
https://www.aiditalia.org/gli-strumenti-compensativi
Information about supports to adapt to the learner and task; not diagnosis or treatment advice.
[SKILL_LIBRARY_PAPER] Kassis, T., Agarwal, V., He, Y., Patel, D., & Brueckner, A. M. (2026). Scientific Agent Skills: A library of procedural knowledge for research agents. [Preprint]. arXiv. https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2609.00065
https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00065
Procedural library documentation; no task-level effectiveness evaluation. Acknowledges the citation-management workflow.
[EDU_SELF_ASSESSMENT] Andrade, H. L. (2019). A critical review of research on student self-assessment. Frontiers in Education, 4, 87.
https://doi.org/10.3389/feduc.2019.00087
Definition and review of 76 empirical studies; not validation of this workshop, its three-step illustration or AI feedback. Andrea’s example is a constructed teaching scenario.
[EDU_SELF_DIRECTED] National Library of Medicine. (n.d.). Self-Directed Learning as Topic (MeSH D000073819). Definition attributed to Knowles (1975), Self-directed learning: A guide for learners and teachers. New York: Association Press.
https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/mesh/2023167
Conceptual definition, not an efficacy trial. Knowles’s definition was verified through the NLM entry; the full original book was not consulted. The three groups and Andrea’s example are workshop illustrations.
